```html
  const GOLDEN_CHANCE = 0.01; // 1 in 100
  const GOLDEN_MULTIPLIER = 100;

  // Achievements: unlocked once, grant a small permanent bonus via toast only.
  const ACHIEVEMENTS = [
    { id: "firstClick", name: "First Click!", test: () => state.totalClicks >= 1 },
    { id: "clicks100", name: "100 Clicks!", test: () => state.totalClicks >= 100 },
    { id: "clicks1000", name: "1,000 Clicks!", test: () => state.totalClicks >= 1000 },
    { id: "score1k", name: "1K Score!", test: () => state.score >= 1000 },
    { id: "score1m", name: "1M Score!", test: () => state.score >= 1e6 },
    { id: "score1b", name: "1B Score!", test: () => state.score >= 1e9 },
    { id: "level5", name: "Level 5!", test: () => state.level >= 5 },
    { id: "level10", name: "Level 10!", test: () => state.level >= 10 },
    { id: "firstRebirth", name: "First Rebirth!", test: () => state.rebirths >= 1 },
    { id: "combo25", name: "25x Combo!", test: () => combo >= 25 },
  ];

  // Milestone rewards: score thresholds that grant a one-time bonus.
  const MILESTONES = [
    { threshold: 1000, reward: 500 },
    { threshold: 10000, reward: 5000 },
    { threshold: 100000, reward: 50000 },
    { threshold: 1e6, reward: 500000 },
    { threshold: 1e7, reward: 5e6 },
    { threshold: 1e8, reward: 5e7 },
    { threshold: 1e9, reward: 5e8 },
  ];

  function showToast(text) {
    const el = document.createElement("div");
    el.className = "toast";
    el.textContent = text;
    toastContainerEl.appendChild(el);
    setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 3000);
  }

  function checkAchievements() {
    for (const a of ACHIEVEMENTS) {
      if (!state.achievements[a.id] && a.test()) {
        state.achievements[a.id] = true;
        showToast("\uD83C\uDFC6 " + a.name);
      }
    }
  }

  function checkMilestones() {
    for (const m of MILESTONES) {
      if (!state.milestones[m.threshold] && state.score >= m.threshold) {
        state.milestones[m.threshold] = true;
        state.score += m.reward;
        showToast("\uD83C\uDF89 Milestone! +" + format(m.reward));
      }
    }
  }
```

```html
  function doClick(x, y) {
    // Combo: rapid clicks build a multiplier.
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - lastClickTime < COMBO_WINDOW_MS) {
      combo = Math.min(COMBO_MAX, combo + 1);
    } else {
      combo = 1;
    }
    lastClickTime = now;
    const comboMult = 1 + combo * 0.02; // +2% per combo step

    let gain = perClick() * comboMult;
    const isGolden = Math.random() < GOLDEN_CHANCE;
    if (isGolden) {
      gain *= GOLDEN_MULTIPLIER;
      clickBtn.classList.add("golden");
      setTimeout(() => clickBtn.classList.remove("golden"), 400);
    }
    showFloat(gain, x, y, isGolden);
    state.score += gain;
    state.totalClicks++;
    addXp(Math.max(1, Math.floor(gain / 10)));
    bumpScore();
    checkMilestones();
    checkAchievements();
    save();
    render();
  }
```

####  Can you improve the Gacha system?  

I'll improve the gacha with several upgrades: a "Roll 100x" button, a visible odds table, a "best pull" tracker, a skip-animation toggle, and a "sell duplicates" style bonus where higher rarities also grant a score bonus. I'll also make the pity counters visible as progress bars.

```html
  function applyRoll() {
    const rarity = rollRarity();

      state.score += rarity.scoreBonus;

      state.mythicPity = 0;
      state.pity = 0;

      state.mythicPity += 1;

      state.bestPull = { rarity: rarity.name, name: u.name, copies: rarity.copies };
    }
```

####  can you make like a even better rebirth not like the one that I have so far like a crazy good one that maybe you can add like other stuff to it or just make it like a completely different thing  

I'll add a second, much more powerful prestige layer called **Ascension** — a separate panel with its own currency (Ascension Shards), a big permanent multiplier, and a shop of permanent perks you can buy with shards. It resets rebirths too, so it's a true "crazy good" tier above rebirth.

Here are the changes:

1. Add CSS for the Ascension panel and perk shop.
2. Add the Ascension panel markup.
3. Add ascension state, shard calculation, and perk definitions.
4. Wire the multiplier into income.
5. Add render + event handlers.

```html
  function rebirthMultiplier() {

  // ---- Ascension (prestige layer above rebirth) ----
  // Requires at least this many rebirths to ascend.
  const ASCEND_REBIRTH_REQ = 5;

  // Shards earned on ascend scale with rebirths and total upgrades owned.
  function shardsOnAscend() {
    if (state.rebirths < ASCEND_REBIRTH_REQ) return 0;
    let owned = 0;
    for (const u of upgrades) owned += state.owned[u.id] || 0;
    return Math.floor(state.rebirths * 2 + Math.sqrt(owned) * 3);
  }

  function canAscend() {
    return state.rebirths >= ASCEND_REBIRTH_REQ;
  }

  // Permanent perks bought with shards.
  const PERKS = [
    { id: "income", name: "Income Boost", desc: "+25% income/lvl", max: 50, baseCost: 1 },
    { id: "click", name: "Click Power", desc: "+50% click/lvl", max: 50, baseCost: 1 },
    { id: "golden", name: "Golden Luck", desc: "+0.5% golden chance/lvl", max: 20, baseCost: 3 },
    { id: "xp", name: "Fast Learner", desc: "+20% XP/lvl", max: 25, baseCost: 2 },
    { id: "start", name: "Head Start", desc: "Start with score/lvl", max: 30, baseCost: 2 },
  ];

  function perkLevel(id) {
    return state.perks[id] || 0;
  }

  function perkCost(perk) {
    return Math.floor(perk.baseCost * Math.pow(1.6, perkLevel(perk.id)));
  }

  function ascensionMultiplier() {
    // Each ascension gives a big flat multiplier, plus income perk.
    const base = Math.pow(2, state.ascensions);
    const perk = 1 + perkLevel("income") * 0.25;
    return base * perk;
  }

  function clickPerkMultiplier() {
    return 1 + perkLevel("click") * 0.5;
  }

  function goldenChance() {
    return GOLDEN_CHANCE + perkLevel("golden") * 0.005;
  }

  function xpPerkMultiplier() {
    return 1 + perkLevel("xp") * 0.2;
  }
```

```html
  function renderAscension() {
    asShardsEl.textContent = format(state.shards) + " shards";
    asMultEl.textContent = "x" + format(ascensionMultiplier()) + " income";

    if (canAscend()) {

      asReqEl.textContent =

      ascendBtn.disabled = false;
      ascendBtn.textContent = "Ascend (+" + format(gain) + ")";
    } else {
      asReqEl.textContent =
        "Reach " + ASCEND_REBIRTH_REQ + " rebirths to ascend (" +
        state.rebirths + "/" + ASCEND_REBIRTH_REQ + ")";
      ascendBtn.disabled = true;
      ascendBtn.textContent = "Ascend";
    }

    // Perk shop
    perkListEl.innerHTML = "";
    for (const perk of PERKS) {
      const lvl = perkLevel(perk.id);
      const cost = perkCost(perk);
      const maxed = lvl >= perk.max;

      const row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "perk";

      const name = document.createElement("span");
      name.className = "perk-name";
      name.textContent = perk.name + " ";
      const lvlSpan = document.createElement("span");
      lvlSpan.className = "perk-lvl";
      lvlSpan.textContent = "Lv" + lvl;
      name.appendChild(lvlSpan);

      const btn = document.createElement("button");
      if (maxed) {
        btn.textContent = "MAX";
        btn.disabled = true;
      } else {
        btn.textContent = cost + " \u25C6";
        btn.disabled = state.shards < cost;
        btn.title = perk.desc;
        btn.addEventListener("click", () => buyPerk(perk));
      }

      row.appendChild(name);
      row.appendChild(btn);
      perkListEl.appendChild(row);
    }
  }

  function buyPerk(perk) {
    const lvl = perkLevel(perk.id);
    if (lvl >= perk.max) return;
    const cost = perkCost(perk);
    if (state.shards < cost) return;
    state.shards -= cost;
    state.perks[perk.id] = lvl + 1;
    save();
    render();
  }
```

#### i made a new Version of my program how to push it to the streamline app  cloud server  

No code changes needed — deploying to Streamlit Community Cloud is done via git. Since you're already in a git repo, just commit and push your new version to GitHub, and Streamlit Cloud will auto-redeploy.

```bash
git add -A && git commit -m "feat: new version"
git push
```

A few things to check:

1. **Your repo is on GitHub** and connected to your Streamlit Cloud app (Streamlit Cloud deploys from a GitHub repo, not from local files).
2. **`requirements.txt` exists** in the repo root listing dependencies (at minimum `streamlit`). If it's missing, create it — otherwise the deploy may fail.
3. **`save_data.json`** — on Streamlit Cloud the filesystem is ephemeral and resets on redeploy/reboot, so saved XP/garden won't persist reliably. If you want persistence, you'd need external storage (e.g. a database or cloud bucket). Let me know if you want help with that.
4. **Auto-redeploy**: by default Streamlit Cloud redeploys on every push to the connected branch. You can also hit **Reboot** in the app's ⋮ menu to force a fresh deploy.

If your app isn't yet connected to Streamlit Cloud, go to https://share.streamlit.io → **New app** → pick your repo, branch, and `app.py` as the main file path.

####  create a new application called kuala  Lumpur pocket  money and allowance tracker  

I'll create a new Streamlit app for tracking pocket money and allowances in Kuala Lumpur, using Malaysian Ringgit (RM) and following the same conventions as your existing app (session state, JSON persistence, sidebar stats).

kuala_lumpur_tracker.py
```python
<<<<<<< SEARCH
=======
import streamlit as st
import json
import os
from datetime import date, datetime

SAVE_FILE = "kl_tracker_data.json"

CATEGORIES = [
    "Allowance",
    "Food",
    "Transport",
    "School",
    "Entertainment",
    "Shopping",
    "Savings",
    "Other",
]

def load_data():
    """Load persisted tracker data from disk, or return defaults."""
    if os.path.exists(SAVE_FILE):
        try:
            with open(SAVE_FILE, "r") as f:
                return json.load(f)
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
            pass
    return {}

def save_data():
    """Persist the current tracker state to disk."""
    data = {
        "balance": st.session_state.get("balance", 0.0),
        "transactions": st.session_state.get("transactions", []),
    }
    try:
        with open(SAVE_FILE, "w") as f:
            json.dump(data, f)
    except OSError:
        pass

def format_rm(amount):
    """Format a number as Malaysian Ringgit."""
    return f"RM {amount:,.2f}"

def add_transaction(kind, amount, category, note):
    """Record a transaction and update the balance."""
    amount = abs(float(amount))
    if kind == "Income":
        st.session_state.balance += amount
        signed = amount
    else:
        st.session_state.balance -= amount
        signed = -amount
    st.session_state.transactions.append(
        {
            "id": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "date": str(date.today()),
            "kind": kind,
            "amount": signed,
            "category": category,
            "note": note.strip(),
        }
    )
    save_data()

def main():
    st.set_page_config(
        page_title="KL Pocket Money Tracker",
        page_icon="💰",
        layout="centered",
    )

    saved = load_data()

    if "balance" not in st.session_state:
        st.session_state.balance = saved.get("balance", 0.0)
    if "transactions" not in st.session_state:
        st.session_state.transactions = saved.get("transactions", [])

    st.title("💰 Kuala Lumpur Pocket Money & Allowance Tracker")
    st.caption("Track your daily allowance and spending in Malaysian Ringgit (RM).")

    # Balance summary
    balance = st.session_state.balance
    st.markdown(
        f"""
        <style>
        .balance-card {{
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #1976d2, #42a5f5);
            color: white;
            padding: 20px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            text-align: center;
            margin-bottom: 16px;
        }}
        .balance-label {{ font-size: 14px; opacity: 0.9; }}
        .balance-value {{ font-size: 40px; font-weight: bold; }}
        </style>
        <div class="balance-card">
            <div class="balance-label">Current Balance</div>
            <div class="balance-value">{format_rm(balance)}</div>
        </div>
        """,
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )

    # Sidebar stats
    with st.sidebar:
        st.header("📊 Summary")
        income = sum(t["amount"] for t in st.session_state.transactions if t["amount"] > 0)
        expenses = sum(-t["amount"] for t in st.session_state.transactions if t["amount"] < 0)
        st.metric("Total Income", format_rm(income))
        st.metric("Total Spent", format_rm(expenses))
        st.metric("Transactions", len(st.session_state.transactions))
        st.divider()
        if st.button("🗑️ Reset all data", use_container_width=True):
            st.session_state.balance = 0.0
            st.session_state.transactions = []
            save_data()
            st.rerun()

    # Add transaction form
    st.subheader("➕ Add Transaction")
    with st.form("add_transaction", clear_on_submit=True):
        col1, col2 = st.columns(2)
        with col1:
            kind = st.selectbox("Type", ["Income", "Expense"])
        with col2:
            amount = st.number_input("Amount (RM)", min_value=0.0, step=0.50, format="%.2f")
        category = st.selectbox("Category", CATEGORIES)
        note = st.text_input("Note (optional)", placeholder="e.g. Nasi lemak at mamak")
        submitted = st.form_submit_button("Add")

        if submitted:
            if amount <= 0:
                st.error("Please enter an amount greater than 0.")
            else:
                add_transaction(kind, amount, category, note)
                st.success(f"Added {kind.lower()}: {format_rm(amount)}")
                st.rerun()

    # Transaction history
    st.subheader("📜 Transaction History")
    if not st.session_state.transactions:
        st.info("No transactions yet. Add your first allowance or expense above!")
    else:
        # Filter by category
        filter_cat = st.selectbox("Filter by category", ["All"] + CATEGORIES)
        rows = st.session_state.transactions
        if filter_cat != "All":
            rows = [t for t in rows if t["category"] == filter_cat]

        # Show newest first
        for t in reversed(rows):
            is_income = t["amount"] > 0
            color = "#2e7d32" if is_income else "#c62828"
            sign = "+" if is_income else "-"
            note = f" — {t['note']}" if t.get("note") else ""
            st.markdown(
                f"""
                <div style="
                    display: flex;
                    justify-content: space-between;
                    align-items: center;
                    padding: 10px 14px;
                    margin-bottom: 6px;
                    border-radius: 8px;
                    border-left: 5px solid {color};
                    background: #fafafa;
                ">
                    <div>
                        <div style="font-weight: bold;">{t['category']}{note}</div>
                        <div style="font-size: 12px; color: #666;">{t['date']}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div style="font-weight: bold; color: {color};">
                        {sign}{format_rm(abs(t['amount']))}
                    </div>
                </div>
                """,
                unsafe_allow_html=True,
            )

        # Delete a transaction
        with st.expander("Delete a transaction"):
            options = {
                f"{t['date']} · {t['category']} · {format_rm(abs(t['amount']))}": i
                for i, t in enumerate(st.session_state.transactions)
            }
            choice = st.selectbox("Select transaction", list(options.keys()))
            if st.button("Delete selected"):
                idx = options[choice]
                removed = st.session_state.transactions.pop(idx)
                # Reverse the balance effect
                st.session_state.balance -= removed["amount"]
                save_data()
                st.rerun()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
>>>>>>> REPLACE
```

```python
def format_rm(amount):
    """Format a number as Malaysian Ringgit."""
    return f"RM {amount:,.2f}"
=======
def format_money(amount_rm):
    """Format an RM amount in the currently selected currency."""
    code = st.session_state.get("currency", "RM")
    cur = CURRENCIES.get(code, CURRENCIES["RM"])
    converted = amount_rm * cur["rate"]
    return f"{cur['symbol']} {converted:,.2f}"

def to_display(amount_rm):
    """Convert an RM amount to the currently selected currency (number only)."""
    code = st.session_state.get("currency", "RM")
    return amount_rm * CURRENCIES.get(code, CURRENCIES["RM"])["rate"]

def from_display(amount_display):
    """Convert an amount in the selected currency back to RM."""
    code = st.session_state.get("currency", "RM")
    rate = CURRENCIES.get(code, CURRENCIES["RM"])["rate"]
    return amount_display / rate if rate else amount_display
```

####  can you save all the codes so if you restart your computer or close the tab, your progress will save  

I'll make the KL tracker persist everything automatically — tasks aren't in this app, but the tracker's balance, transactions, savings goal, currency, and celebration flag already save on change. The gaps are: the **task list** in `app.py` isn't persisted, and the tracker's `save_data()` is only called on specific actions. I'll add auto-save to both apps so state survives restarts and tab closes.

####  the coins are not appearing even when I hit my goal why?  

The bug: the coin row is rendered **before** the sidebar runs, so when the goal is reached and `coins += 1` happens in the sidebar, the coin row has already been drawn for that rerun. The coin only appears on the *next* rerun — and since the sidebar doesn't trigger one, it looks like nothing happened.

Fix: move the coin row rendering to **after** the sidebar block, so it reflects the updated coin count in the same run.

####  can you build some advancements?  

"Advancements" is a bit ambiguous — could you clarify what you'd like? A few options for the KL tracker:

1. **Achievements/badges** — unlock milestones (e.g. "First 100 earned", "5 trophies", "Saved RM 500") shown in a gallery.
2. **Levels/rank** — earn XP from transactions and level up with a progress bar.
3. **Streaks** — track consecutive days of logging transactions.
4. **Charts** — spending breakdown by category (pie/bar) and balance over time.
5. **Budgets** — set a monthly limit per category with warnings when exceeded.
6. **Recurring transactions** — auto-add weekly allowance.

Which of these (or something else) do you mean by "advancements"? If you just want me to pick, I'd suggest **achievements/badges** since it fits the coin/trophy theme nicely.

####  build a place so you can see all your  advancement  

I'll add an **Advancements** page to the KL tracker that shows all achievements — coins earned, trophies collected, and milestone badges — in one place. I'll add a page switcher in the sidebar and a new `advancements_page()` function.

kuala_lumpur_tracker.py
```python
<<<<<<< SEARCH
def main():
    st.set_page_config(
        page_title="KL Pocket Money Tracker",
        page_icon="💰",
        layout="centered",
    )
=======
# Milestone badges: (id, label, emoji, description, condition)
def get_advancements():
    """Return a list of advancement dicts with unlocked status."""
    coins = st.session_state.get("coins", 0)
    trophies = st.session_state.get("trophies", [])
    txns = st.session_state.get("transactions", [])
    income = sum(t["amount"] for t in txns if t["amount"] > 0)
    expenses = sum(-t["amount"] for t in txns if t["amount"] < 0)
    balance = st.session_state.get("balance", 0.0)

    defs = [
        ("first_txn", "First Step", "👣", "Log your first transaction", len(txns) >= 1),
        ("first_income", "Allowance Day", "💵", "Record your first income", income > 0),
        ("first_expense", "Spender", "🛒", "Record your first expense", expenses > 0),
        ("first_coin", "First Coin", "🪙", "Earn your first gold coin", coins >= 1),
        ("coins_5", "Coin Collector", "💰", "Earn 5 gold coins", coins >= 5),
        ("coins_10", "Coin Hoarder", "🏦", "Earn 10 gold coins", coins >= 10),
        ("first_trophy", "Goal Getter", "🏆", "Reach your first savings goal", len(trophies) >= 1),
        ("trophies_3", "Trophy Hunter", "🎖️", "Collect 3 trophies", len(trophies) >= 3),
        ("trophies_5", "Champion", "👑", "Collect 5 trophies", len(trophies) >= 5),
        ("balance_100", "Saver", "🌱", "Reach a balance of 100", balance >= 100),
        ("balance_500", "Big Saver", "🌳", "Reach a balance of 500", balance >= 500),
        ("balance_1000", "Money Master", "💎", "Reach a balance of 1000", balance >= 1000),
        ("txns_10", "Consistent", "📈", "Log 10 transactions", len(txns) >= 10),
        ("txns_50", "Dedicated", "🔥", "Log 50 transactions", len(txns) >= 50),
    ]
    return [
        {"id": i, "label": l, "emoji": e, "desc": d, "unlocked": bool(c)}
        for (i, l, e, d, c) in defs
    ]


def advancements_page():
    st.title("🏅 Advancements")
    st.caption("All your achievements in one place — coins, trophies, and milestone badges.")

    advancements = get_advancements()
    unlocked = [a for a in advancements if a["unlocked"]]
    total = len(advancements)
    pct = len(unlocked) / total if total else 0.0

    st.progress(pct, text=f"{len(unlocked)} / {total} advancements unlocked")

    # Summary metrics
    c1, c2, c3 = st.columns(3)
    c1.metric("🪙 Coins", st.session_state.get("coins", 0))
    c2.metric("🏆 Trophies", len(st.session_state.get("trophies", [])))
    c3.metric("🏅 Badges", f"{len(unlocked)}/{total}")

    st.divider()

    # Badge grid
    st.subheader("Milestone Badges")
    badge_html = ""
    for a in advancements:
        if a["unlocked"]:
            style = (
                "background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fff8e1, #ffe0b2);"
                "border: 2px solid #ffb300;"
                "box-shadow: 0 3px 10px rgba(255,179,0,0.4);"
            )
            icon_style = "filter: none;"
            label_color = "#b8860b"
        else:
            style = (
                "background: #f5f5f5;"
                "border: 2px dashed #bdbdbd;"
                "opacity: 0.6;"
            )
            icon_style = "filter: grayscale(1);"
            label_color = "#9e9e9e"
        badge_html += (
            f"<div class='badge' style='{style}'>"
            f"<div class='badge-icon' style='{icon_style}'>{a['emoji']}</div>"
            f"<div class='badge-label' style='color:{label_color};'>{a['label']}</div>"
            f"<div class='badge-desc'>{a['desc']}</div>"
            f"</div>"
        )
    st.markdown(
        f"""
        <style>
        .badge-grid {{
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(140px, 1fr));
            gap: 12px;
            margin-top: 8px;
        }}
        .badge {{
            text-align: center;
            padding: 14px 10px;
            border-radius: 12px;
        }}
        .badge-icon {{ font-size: 36px; line-height: 1; }}
        .badge-label {{ font-weight: bold; margin-top: 6px; font-size: 14px; }}
        .badge-desc {{ font-size: 11px; color: #757575; margin-top: 2px; }}
        </style>
        <div class="badge-grid">{badge_html}</div>
        """,
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )

    st.divider()

    # Trophy room (reused view)
    st.subheader("🏆 Trophy Room")
    trophies = st.session_state.get("trophies", [])
    if not trophies:
        st.info("No trophies yet — reach a savings goal to earn one!")
    else:
        trophy_html = "".join(
            f"<div class='trophy'>"
            f"<div class='trophy-icon'>🏆</div>"
            f"<div class='trophy-goal'>{t['currency']} {t['goal']:,.2f}</div>"
            f"<div class='trophy-date'>{t['date']}</div>"
            f"</div>"
            for t in trophies
        )
        st.markdown(
            f"""
            <style>
            .trophy-shelf {{
                display: flex;
                flex-wrap: nowrap;
                gap: 12px;
                padding: 16px;
                border-radius: 12px;
                background: linear-gradient(180deg, #fff8e1, #ffe0b2);
                border: 2px solid #ffb300;
                overflow-x: auto;
                overflow-y: hidden;
                scroll-behavior: smooth;
                -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
            }}
            .trophy {{
                flex: 0 0 auto;
                text-align: center;
                padding: 12px 16px;
                border-radius: 10px;
                background: white;
                border: 2px solid #ffd740;
                box-shadow: 0 3px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.15);
                min-width: 100px;
            }}
            .trophy-icon {{ font-size: 40px; line-height: 1; }}
            .trophy-goal {{ font-weight: bold; color: #b8860b; margin-top: 4px; }}
            .trophy-date {{ font-size: 12px; color: #8d6e63; }}
            </style>
            <div class="trophy-shelf">{trophy_html}</div>
            """,
            unsafe_allow_html=True,
        )


def main():
    st.set_page_config(
        page_title="KL Pocket Money Tracker",
        page_icon="💰",
        layout="centered",
    )
```

```python
def advancements_page():
=======
def shop_page():
    st.title("🛍️ What Can I Buy?")
    st.caption("Popular items and what your current balance can afford.")

    balance_display = to_display(st.session_state.balance)
    st.markdown(
        f"""
        <div style="
            text-align: center;
            padding: 16px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #1976d2, #42a5f5);
            color: white;
            margin-bottom: 16px;
        ">
            <div style="font-size: 14px; opacity: 0.9;">Your balance</div>
            <div style="font-size: 32px; font-weight: bold;">{format_money(st.session_state.balance)}</div>
        </div>
        """,
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )

    # Filter by category
    shop_categories = sorted({item["category"] for item in SHOP_ITEMS})
    filter_cat = st.selectbox("Filter by category", ["All"] + shop_categories)
    items = SHOP_ITEMS if filter_cat == "All" else [i for i in SHOP_ITEMS if i["category"] == filter_cat]

    # Sort by price ascending so affordable items appear first
    items = sorted(items, key=lambda i: i["price"])

    affordable = [i for i in items if to_display(i["price"]) <= balance_display]
    st.write(f"You can afford **{len(affordable)}** of {len(items)} items shown.")

    # Render item cards
    card_html = ""
    for item in items:
        price_display = to_display(item["price"])
        can_afford = price_display <= balance_display
        if can_afford:
            style = (
                "background: linear-gradient(135deg, #e8f5e9, #c8e6c9);"
                "border: 2px solid #43a047;"
            )
            badge = "<span style='color:#2e7d32;font-weight:bold;'>✔ Affordable</span>"
        else:
            style = (
                "background: #f5f5f5;"
                "border: 2px dashed #bdbdbd;"
                "opacity: 0.7;"
            )
            badge = "<span style='color:#9e9e9e;'>🔒 Need more</span>"
        card_html += (
            f"<div class='shop-card' style='{style}'>"
            f"<div class='shop-emoji'>{item['emoji']}</div>"
            f"<div class='shop-name'>{item['name']}</div>"
            f"<div class='shop-price'>{format_money(item['price'])}</div>"
            f"<div class='shop-badge'>{badge}</div>"
            f"</div>"
        )

    st.markdown(
        f"""
        <style>
        .shop-grid {{
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(150px, 1fr));
            gap: 12px;
            margin-top: 8px;
        }}
```

####  OK here's a hard question. Can you add a different things or remove different things in different money zones  

I need to clarify what you mean by "money zones" before making changes. A few interpretations:

1. **Per-currency shop catalogs** — different items available depending on the selected currency (e.g. RM shows Malaysian food, USD shows American items, JPY shows Japanese items).
2. **Regional zones** — group items by country/region (Malaysia, USA, Japan, etc.) with a zone selector.
3. **Price tiers** — "zones" as budget ranges (cheap / mid / expensive) with different items in each.
4. **Separate wallets** — split your balance into zones (e.g. "Spending", "Savings", "Fun") each with its own items.

Which one do you mean? If you're not sure, I'd suggest **option 1 (per-currency shop catalogs)** since it fits the existing currency switcher nicely — each currency shows items popular in that country.

####  what's a budget summary chart?  

A **budget summary chart** is a visual breakdown of where your money goes — typically a pie or bar chart showing spending per category (Food, Transport, School, etc.) over a period, so you can see at a glance which categories eat most of your allowance.

For your tracker it would look like:

- **Pie chart** — each slice = a category, sized by total spent in that category.
- **Bar chart** — categories on the x-axis, total spent on the y-axis (easier to compare).
- **Summary line** — total income vs. total spent vs. remaining balance.

It's different from your existing "Summary" metrics (which just show totals) because it shows the *distribution* across categories.

Would you like me to add one? If so, I'd suggest a **bar chart of spending by category** plus a small income-vs-expense summary, using Streamlit's built-in `st.bar_chart` (no new dependencies needed). Just say the word and I'll add it to `kuala_lumpur_tracker.py`.

####  Can you add a budget chart to my app?

```python
    # Add transaction form

    # Budget summary chart: spending by category
    st.subheader("📊 Budget Summary")
    if not st.session_state.transactions:
        st.info("Add some transactions to see your budget breakdown.")
    else:
        # Aggregate spending per category (in current display currency)
        spend_by_cat = {}
        for t in st.session_state.transactions:
            if t["amount"] < 0:
                cat = t["category"]
                spend_by_cat[cat] = spend_by_cat.get(cat, 0.0) + to_display(-t["amount"])

        total_income = sum(t["amount"] for t in st.session_state.transactions if t["amount"] > 0)
        total_spent = sum(-t["amount"] for t in st.session_state.transactions if t["amount"] < 0)

        m1, m2, m3 = st.columns(3)
        m1.metric("Income", format_money(total_income))
        m2.metric("Spent", format_money(total_spent))
        m3.metric("Balance", format_money(st.session_state.balance))

        if spend_by_cat:
            st.caption(f"Spending by category ({st.session_state.currency})")
            st.bar_chart(spend_by_cat)
        else:
            st.caption("No expenses recorded yet — your spending chart will appear here.")

    # Add transaction form
    st.subheader("➕ Add Transaction")
```

```python
            n = 2

                n += 1
```
